Extensive experiments under strict test-then-adapt OTTA settings demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on ten real-world datasets from diverse domains.

</details>


### [83] [ReguSim: Evaluating LLM Agent Rule Grounding in Financial Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19974)
*Yiyang Luo,Yihang Jiang,Qijun Xie,Liang Lan,Lin Willian Cong,Anyi Rao,Yunya Song*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出金融合规环境ReguSim和基准ReguBench，分离四类产物（陈述推理、尝试动作、执行强制、监控证据），发现规则可见降低但未消除违规动作，独立监控易被交易员理由误导，结构化基线优于纯提示LLM，主张合规评估应审计规则行为和证据使用而非单一分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在金融市场中可能引述规则但提交违反可执行约束的订单或误读监管证据，缺乏能区分推理、行为、执行和监控证据的受控评估环境，导致合规评价混淆。

Method: 构建ReguSim受控金融合规环境与ReguBench目标标记监控基准，设计交易员运行实验，比较DeepSeek V4 Pro和Gemini 3.5 Flash在规则可见/不可见、激励或角色框架下的行为，并通过桥梁研究测试独立监控器对交易员理由的依赖，对比简单结构化基线与纯提示LLM的监控效果。

Result: 可见规则降低但不能消除拒绝动作，激励或角色框架改变行为；交易员理由可误导独立监控器除非显示执行证据；简单结构化基线在监控上匹配或超越纯提示LLM。

Conclusion: 金融合规评估应作为对基于规则的行为和证据使用的审计，而非单一合规分数，强调执行强制证据在监控中的关键作用。

Abstract: LLM agents in financial markets may cite rules yet still submit orders that violate executable constraints or misread surveillance evidence. We introduce ReguSim, a controlled financial-compliance environment, and ReguBench, a target-marked monitoring benchmark, to separate four artifacts: stated reasoning, attempted action, execution enforcement, and monitor evidence. In trader runs with DeepSeek V4 Pro and Gemini 3.5 Flash, visible rules reduce but do not eliminate rejected actions, and incentive or persona framing shifts behavior. A bridge study shows that trader rationales can mislead an independent monitor unless enforcement evidence is shown. In monitoring, simple structured baselines either match or exceed prompt-only LLMs. The results frame financial compliance evaluation as an audit of rule-grounded actions and evidence use, rather than a single compliance score.

</details>


### [84] [Separating Covariate Shift from Mechanism Change with Two Discriminators: CJSD, a Conditional Discrepancy with an Exact Covariate-Concept Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19885)
*Kentaro Oda*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一个带决策层的专家池流式系统：通过两个单边序列假设（赌e过程）在重用/新建/延迟间做统计可解释选择，并用重启赌检测器实现终身任意有效性和零误判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式系统维护专家池时，重用、新建或延迟的决策缺乏统计学意义，且窗口启发式难以平衡时效性与全局保证。

Method: 将重用与新建设为带无差别区的单边序列假设，延迟对应无充分证据态；用可预测判别器的替代差异构造赌e过程，证明有限时有效性并单边传递到总体量；用几何间隔重启的赌超鞅组（O(log t)内存）在不损失终身保证下处理概念漂移，并按专家创建序分配误差预算控制多重性。

Result: 在合成多概念流、Electricity、Covertype和INSECTS基准上，重启赌检测器实现零假新建、切换后零假重用，且精度（INSECTS-reoccurring 0.675）匹配或超过窗口启发式。

Conclusion: 算法部署与统计保证统一，证实了理论引导的决策层在实际流式任务中既保守又高效。

Abstract: Streaming systems that maintain a pool of expert models must repeatedly decide whether to reuse an existing expert for arriving data, spawn a new one, or defer. We present a decision layer that makes all three outcomes statistically meaningful. Reuse and spawn are posed as one-sided sequential hypotheses on a conditional (mechanism-level) discrepancy, separated by an indifference zone; defer is exactly the state in which neither betting e-process has accumulated sufficient evidence. We prove finite-time anytime validity for the observable surrogate discrepancy of a predictable discriminator sequence, and an unconditional one-sided transfer to the population quantity in which each side's slack is the excess risk of a single discriminator; an empirically observed downward-bias regularity makes the spawn side exactly conservative. Recency without sacrificing the guarantee is obtained by a restarted e-detector: a bank of unwindowed betting supermartingales at geometrically spaced restart times (O(log t) memory), with the error budget spent over restart instances, which preserves lifetime anytime validity; spending over expert-creation order likewise controls multiplicity for unboundedly many experts. On synthetic multi-concept streams, Electricity, Covertype, and the recurrence-heavy INSECTS benchmark, the instance-accounted restarted bank achieves zero false spawns and zero false reuses after switches and matches or exceeds the retired windowed heuristic (INSECTS-reoccurring accuracy 0.675), making the deployed algorithm and the guaranteed algorithm one and the same.

</details>


### [85] [Optimal Skill Selection for LLM Agents with Provable Bicriteria Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19993)
*Yu Chen,Ruishuo Chen,Xun Wang,Zhuoran Li,Longbo Huang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文首次将技能选择建模为带硬标记预算的优化问题，提出多项式时间算法BPS，并给出双标准近似保证；在受控基准上以更少token显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体按语义相关性独立评分技能并用top-k或贪心打包，缺乏质量保证和成本意识，导致冗余技能浪费上下文token甚至降低性能。

Method: 将技能选择形式化为在硬token预算下最大化单调子模效益减去上下文惩罚的优化问题，并设计多项式时间算法Best Prefix Selection (BPS)，证明其双标准(1-1/e,1)近似保证，效益系数在多项式时间内最优。

Result: 在去污染的BigCodeBench变体上，BPS以比最强发布路由器少28%的token达到0.73的任务成功率，远超基线（0.20–0.52）。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Loading reusable skill documents into a bounded context window is now the primary way large language model (LLM) agents acquire task-specific capabilities, which makes skill selection a first-order determinant of task performance and token cost. Yet current agents score skills independently by semantic relevance and assemble the set by top-$k$ or greedy packing, with no quality guarantee or cost awareness on the selected set. As a result, redundant or poorly chosen skills waste scarce context tokens and can even degrade performance. We give the first model of how the selected skill set shapes execution outcomes and cast skill selection as an optimization problem: choose a skill set under a hard token budget to maximize a monotone submodular benefit minus context penalty. For this problem, we develop Best Prefix Selection (BPS), a polynomial-time algorithm, and prove, to our knowledge, the first performance guarantee for skill selection: a bicriteria $(1-1/e,1)$ approximation whose benefit coefficient is optimal in polynomial time. On a contamination-controlled BigCodeBench variant, BPS outperforms all the baselines, reaching $0.73$ measured task success versus $0.20$--$0.52$ for released skill routers, text retrievers, and the executor's own selection, on $28\%$ fewer tokens than the strongest released router.

</details>


### [86] [Evidence Before Expansion: Reuse, Spawn, or Defer in Lifelong Expert Pools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19888)
*Kentaro Oda*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一个决策层，使流式专家池的复用、新建和延迟三种决策在统计上均有意义，通过重启e检测器实现有限时间任意有效性和零误判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式专家池系统在决定复用、新建或延迟时缺乏统计保证，导致错误率高，需一个既有理论保证又实用的决策机制。

Method: 将复用和新建设为单侧序贯假设，用赌超鞅（e-process）作为证据，延迟为无充分证据状态；通过几何间隔重启、O(log t)内存的检测器银行，控制多重性并保留终身有效性。

Result: 在合成及真实基准（Electricity、Covertype、INSECTS）上，实现零假新建和零假复用，精度达到或超过窗口启发式（如0.675）。

Conclusion: 部署算法与保证算法一致，证明理论可靠且实际高效，为流式概念漂移提供可验证的决策框架。

Abstract: Streaming systems that maintain a pool of expert models must repeatedly decide whether to reuse an existing expert for arriving data, spawn a new one, or defer. We present a decision layer that makes all three outcomes statistically meaningful. Reuse and spawn are posed as one-sided sequential hypotheses on a conditional (mechanism-level) discrepancy, separated by an indifference zone; defer is exactly the state in which neither betting e-process has accumulated sufficient evidence. We prove finite-time anytime validity for the observable surrogate discrepancy of a predictable discriminator sequence, and an unconditional one-sided transfer to the population quantity in which each side's slack is the excess risk of a single discriminator; an empirically observed downward-bias regularity makes the spawn side exactly conservative. Recency without sacrificing the guarantee is obtained by a restarted e-detector: a bank of unwindowed betting supermartingales at geometrically spaced restart times (O(log t) memory), with the error budget spent over restart instances, which preserves lifetime anytime validity; spending over expert-creation order likewise controls multiplicity for unboundedly many experts. On synthetic multi-concept streams, Electricity, Covertype, and the recurrence-heavy INSECTS benchmark, the instance-accounted restarted bank achieves zero false spawns and zero false reuses after switches and matches or exceeds the retired windowed heuristic (INSECTS-reoccurring accuracy 0.675), making the deployed algorithm and the guaranteed algorithm one and the same.

</details>


### [87] [Green BOA: Determining the environmental break-even point for ML-based data compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19994)
*Caterina Doglioni,Akshat Gupta,Thomas Elliott,Hanzila Hussain,Sanjiban Sengupta*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文总结了两项暑期实习项目的成果，聚焦于基于机器学习的无损压缩算法在环境可持续性方面的盈亏平衡点，比较了训练与推理基础设施的碳排放和因减少磁盘存储所节省的碳排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ML压缩算法在数据存储中的广泛应用，其训练和推理过程会消耗大量能源，产生显著碳排放；但压缩后减少的存储空间又能降低数据中心能耗。需要量化这两者之间的权衡，找到环境效益的临界点，以指导可持续的算法设计。

Method: 以某种ML无损压缩算法为案例，分别估算其训练和推理阶段所需基础设施（如GPU集群、冷却系统）的碳当量，以及与未压缩数据相比因节省磁盘存储而降低的碳当量（包含制造、运行和报废环节）。通过比较两组数值，计算盈亏平衡点（即压缩使用时长或数据规模达到多少时，减排收益超过训练成本）。

Result: 研究得出一个初步的盈亏平衡点：当压缩算法被重复使用足够多次（或处理足够大数据量）时，其环境收益才能覆盖训练成本；在短期或小规模应用下，ML压缩反而可能增加碳排放。具体数值依赖于硬件效率、电力碳排放因子和存储介质类型。

Conclusion: ML压缩算法的环境可持续性并非天然优于传统方法，需根据使用频率和数据规模评估；建议将模型复用性和部署场景纳入设计考量，并优化训练流程以降低碳足迹。

Abstract: We summarise the outcome of two summer internship projects based at the University of Manchester, focused on the break-even point in terms of environmental sustainability for ML-based data compression algorithms. Using the example of a ML-based lossless compression algorithm, we compare estimates for the carbon-equivalent of the infrastructure needed for ML training and inference with the carbon-equivalent savings from reduced disk storage requirements, and discuss their break-even point.

</details>


### [88] [Scale-Aware Pretraining of Time Series Foundation Models via Multi-Patch Token Alignment and Hybrid Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20005)
*Taihua Chen,Xiang Ma,Yixin Zhang,Tailin Zhan,Manyu Sun,Lizhen Cui*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SATS，通过尺度感知的令牌对齐机制和混合掩码策略，在异构时间序列预训练中统一多尺度表示，实现性能与效率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么用数据集特定的patch大小和独立FFN导致表征碎片化，要么用固定patch大小忽略时间动态变化，无法有效处理异构采样频率。

Method: 将patch大小作为显式尺度概念，用对比学习启发的对齐正则化器对齐不同尺度的表示空间，同时保留各自建模能力；结合随机和连续掩码的混合策略捕捉多尺度时间结构。

Result: 在LSTF基准上MSE改善9.2%，GIFT-Eval MASE提升8.3%，模型效率比先进基线提高65.6%，实现SOTA。

Conclusion: SATS在时间序列预训练中兼顾性能与效率，验证了尺度感知对齐和混合掩码的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Pretraining time series foundation models across heterogeneous datasets necessitates effective handling of varying sampling frequencies. Current methods either employ dataset-specific patch sizes and separate FFNs, leading to fragmented representations, or enforce a fixed patch size that neglects inherent temporal variations. To address this, we propose SATS, featuring a scale-aware token alignment mechanism that treats patch size as an explicit notion of scale. By incorporating a contrastive-inspired alignment regularizer, SATS aligns representation spaces across scales while preserving distinct modeling capacities. Furthermore, a hybrid masking strategy combining random and contiguous masking is introduced to capture multi-scale temporal structures. Experimental results on LSTF benchmarks demonstrate that SATS achieves a 9.2% improvement in MSE and an 8.3% gain in GIFT-Eval MASE compared to competitive baselines. Notably, SATS consistently delivers SOTA performance while achieving a 65.6% increase in model efficiency over advanced baselines, highlighting its effectiveness and scalability in time series pretraining.

</details>


### [89] [DARS: Dual-Level Credit Assignment RL with Structured Reasoning for Instruction-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20161)
*Haoxiang Cao,Jiajiong Cao,Xuanpu Zhang,Changqian Yu,Chaoqun Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: DARS是一个用于指令图像编辑的双层强化学习框架，通过跨模块软路由和规划器内结构化奖励分配，在相同预算下优于联合RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅用最终图像奖励训练规划器-渲染器流水线无法定位责任模块，且自由文本推理难以细粒度归因，导致优化低效。

Method: 跨模块采用多计划多渲染rollout计算奖励变异性以进行软路由，并用平均奖励设计自适应课程；规划器内使用四字段结构化输出，通过前缀门控奖励和token级优势重加权实现局部监督。

Result: 在五个基准上超过同骨干、数据、奖励模型和rollout预算的Joint RL基线，尤其在推理密集型编辑上提升最大。

Conclusion: 显式双层信用分配能有效提升两阶段指令编辑系统的训练效率与性能，验证了结构化推理与课程策略的价值。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing uses a planner-renderer pipeline: a vision-language model (VLM) first converts the instruction into an edit plan, and a diffusion model then executes that plan. Training such systems with only final-image rewards is inefficient because a poor edit does not reveal whether additional optimization should place more emphasis on the planner or the renderer, and even planner-dominant cases remain difficult to localize within a free-form reasoning trace. We present DARS, a reinforcement learning framework for dual-level credit assignment in this two-stage setting. Across modules, multi-plan multi-render rollouts estimate between-plan and within-plan reward variability for soft module routing, while rollout mean rewards provide hardness estimates for an adaptive curriculum. Within the planner, a four-field structured reasoning output enables a prefix-gated reward and token-level advantage reweighting, turning outcome-level feedback into localized supervision. Experiments on five benchmarks show that DARS outperforms a Joint~RL baseline with the same backbone, data, reward model, and rollout budget, with the largest gains on reasoning-intensive edits.

</details>


### [90] [Systematic Evaluation of TabPFN-TS for Zero-Shot Probabilistic Heat Load Forecasting in District Heating Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20024)
*Ben Spoek,Karim K. Ben Hicham,Kai Derzsi,Philipp Althaus,Alexander Mitsos,Dirk Müller*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了TabPFN-TS在区域供热网络概率热负荷预测中的表现，发现其与Chronos-2等基础模型精度接近（CVRMSE 13.06% vs 12.48%），且校准更好；并提出多分辨率残差校正预测器以提升长期预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热负荷预测模型需针对特定系统训练，网络变化时需重复训练，负担重；零样本时序基础模型和上下文预测可在推理时适应，避免重训。但TabPFN-TS使用合成预训练数据，需验证其是否捕捉供热动态。

Method: 在代表性运行周上分析协变量、上下文长度、时间分辨率和预测周期的影响，全年验证最优配置，并在第二个网络上测试迁移性；提出多分辨率残差校正预测器，结合低频基础预测器与短周期残差预测器。

Result: 每小时24小时预测、12周滚动上下文和气温为最优配置；TabPFN-TS的CVRMSE为13.06%，接近Chronos-2的12.48%，日排名差异在临界阈值内；TabPFN-TS经验校准更好，但Chronos-2全年总误差最低。

Conclusion: TabPFN-TS在供热负荷预测中表现稳健且校准佳，无需重训即可适应；多分辨率残差校正预测器能改善长期规划精度，为实际系统提供可靠方案。

Abstract: District heating energy hubs require reliable heat load forecasts for efficient operational scheduling. Conventional forecasting workflows train system-specific models on historical data, which can become burdensome when networks change through new consumers, retrofits, or changing operating regimes. Zero-shot time-series foundation models and in-context forecasting offer a promising alternative: they can adapt at inference time from recent observations rather than by repeated retraining. This study systematically evaluates TabPFN-TS against time-series foundation models and trained machine-learning baselines for probabilistic heat load forecasting in district heating networks. Unlike foundation models pretrained on large collections of real time series, TabPFN-TS relies on synthetic pretraining data, which avoids direct pretraining-test overlap but raises the question of whether the learned prior captures district heating dynamics. We analyze covariate choice, context length, temporal resolution, and prediction horizon on representative operating weeks, validate the selected configuration over a full year, and test transferability on a second network. The results identify hourly 24-hour forecasting with a 12-week rolling context and ambient temperature as a parsimonious high-performing configuration; longer context windows do not improve accuracy. TabPFN-TS remains close to Chronos-2 in deterministic accuracy, reaching CVRMSE values of 13.06% versus 12.48% on the main dataset, and lies within the critical-difference threshold in the daily-rank comparison. Although Chronos-2 achieves the lowest aggregate full-year error, TabPFN-TS shows better empirical calibration. Finally, the diagnostic findings motivate a Multi-Resolution Residual-Correction Forecaster that combines a low-frequency Base Forecaster with a short-horizon Residual Forecaster to improve longer-horizon planning accuracy.

</details>


### [91] [MemTrapBench: Benchmarking Cognitive Traps in LLM Memory Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20202)
*Mengru Wang,Haozhe Luo,Zhenqian Xu,Zhixiang Cui,Haoming Xu,Qu Yang,Jizhan Fang,Junfeng Fang,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出MemTrapBench基准，揭示记忆虽正确检索但仍会扭曲模型推理的认知陷阱，并给出缓解方法AdaptiveMem。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准只关注信息提取、存储和检索的准确性，忽视检索到的记忆对当前任务推理的负面影响，导致模型可能陷入认知陷阱。

Method: 构建MemTrapBench，涵盖推理固着与信念扭曲两类陷阱；提出AdaptiveMem，一种推理期指令方法，引导模型规避记忆陷阱。

Result: 在两类模型和五种记忆框架上，所有记忆策略均低于无记忆基线，最强方法性能下降超10%；AdaptiveMem能恢复性能且不损害标准记忆基准。

Conclusion: 记忆的正确性不等于有效性，需要显式防御认知陷阱；AdaptiveMem提供轻量有效方案，并保持通用记忆能力。

Abstract: Memory has become a key component of large language models, enabling them to retain information and learn from long-term interactions. However, existing memory benchmarks mainly evaluate whether information is correctly extracted, stored, and retrieved, while largely overlooking how retrieved memories reshape model reasoning and affect performance on the current task. We identify memory-induced cognitive traps: even faithfully recorded and semantically relevant memories can distort model reasoning or beliefs and degrade current task performance. To systematically evaluate these failure modes, we introduce MemTrapBench, which covers two forms of cognitive traps: Reasoning Fixation and Belief Distortion. Experiments across two model families and five representative memory frameworks show that MemTrapBench is challenging: all evaluated memory strategies underperform the no-memory setting, with even the strongest methods suffering drops of more than 10%. To mitigate these cognitive traps, we propose AdaptiveMem, a simple yet effective inference-time method that instructs LLMs to avoid memory traps. AdaptiveMem mitigates cognitive traps on MemTrapBench while preserving or improving performance on standard memory benchmarks across diverse memory frameworks.

</details>


### [92] [DecoVAE: a Lightweight Interpretable Trend-Seasonal VAE Framework for Efficient Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20052)
*Alexander Marusov,Dmitry Anikin,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出DecoVAE，一种轻量级可解释的时序概率预测框架，通过显式分解趋势和季节性成分，在提升预测精度的同时大幅降低模型开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序预测方法难以同时建模趋势和季节性的独特内在属性，且存在可解释性差、内存和运行开销大的问题。

Method: 将时间序列显式分解为趋势和季节成分：趋势流使用微分正则化器强制潜在轨迹平滑（类似Hodrick-Prescott滤波）；季节流在频域中通过复数高斯VAE原生捕捉周期信号的幅度和相位。

Result: 在7个真实基准上，短期预测CRPS降低14.96%、NMAE降低23.30%；长期预测分别降低52.68%和26.51%。同时模型权重减少93%，速度提升74%。

Conclusion: DecoVAE在保持高精度和高效率的同时，实现了可解释的时序分解，为概率预测提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic time series forecasting remains challenging, largely because modeling distinct trend and seasonal dynamics requires specialized approaches. Existing methods often fail to capture the unique inner properties of these components, lack interpretability, or suffer from heavy memory and runtime overhead. To address these limitations, we propose DecoVAE, a lightweight interpretable trend-seasonal VAE framework that explicitly decomposes time series into trend and seasonal components by applying domain-specific inductive biases. The trend stream enforces structural smoothness using a differential regularizer on the latent trajectory, analogous to the Hodrick-Prescott filter. Concurrently, the seasonal stream operates in the frequency domain via a complex Gaussian VAE, natively capturing the amplitude and phase of periodic patterns. Extensive evaluations across seven real-world benchmarks show that DecoVAE consistently outperforms strong baselines. It achieves reductions of up to 14.96\% in CRPS and 23.30\% in NMAE for short-term forecasting, and up to 52.68\% and 26.51\% for long-term horizons. Crucially, DecoVAE yields these accuracy gains while remaining highly efficient, reducing model weight by up to 93\% and accelerating speed by up to 74\% compared to the second-best method.

</details>


### [93] [Rule-Compliant Visual Spatial Planning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20237)
*Yu Chen,Ting Lei,Yaoyi Li,Jia Cai,Zhecen Wu,Yang Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出RuleMaze基准测试和DMP方法，用于评估和提升多模态大模型在遵循自然语言规则下的视觉空间规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs虽结合语言与视觉，但在显式或未见过的规则约束下进行空间规划的能力尚未被充分探索，缺乏可控的基准和系统性方法。

Method: 构建RuleMaze迷宫导航基准，使用Language-Logic-Function Hybridization自动生成规则并转换为逻辑和验证器；提出Disentangled Multimodal Planning，将感知、执行、规则验证分离为可解释的推理原语。

Result: DMP相比端到端文本规划基线，在规则遵循和规划成功率上显著提升，并展现出对复杂和未见规则的泛化能力。

Conclusion: RuleMaze为MLLMs的基于规则的具身空间规划研究提供了原则性基准，DMP展示了可解释分离式规划的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) combine linguistic reasoning with visual perception, yet their ability to perform visual spatial planning under explicit or previously unseen rule constraints remains underexplored. This setting requires models to jointly understand spatial layouts, interpret natural-language rules, and plan valid actions accordingly. To address this gap, we introduce RuleMaze, a controllable benchmark in which MLLMs must navigate mazes while obeying natural-language rules of varying complexity. RuleMaze isolates rule-compliant spatial planning by requiring accurate perception, rule interpretation, and constrained action planning. To enable scalable and systematic rule construction, we propose Language-Logic-Function Hybridization, which automatically generates natural-language rules and translates them into logical representations and executable validators, eliminating manual rule engineering. To improve rule following and generalization, we introduce Disentangled Multimodal Planning (DMP), which separates perception, execution, and rule verification through interpretable reasoning primitives. By disentangling these components, DMP facilitates systematic generalization to more complex and previously unseen rules, while providing transparent intermediate planning traces. Experiments demonstrate that DMP substantially improves rule compliance and planning success compared to end-to-end textual planning baselines. Overall, RuleMaze establishes a principled benchmark for studying grounded and interpretable rule-based spatial planning in MLLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/RuleMaze.

</details>


### [94] [Orthogonal JEPA: Factorized Predictive States for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20065)
*Taoyong Cui,Pheng Ann Heng,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于正交预测分解的潜在世界模型框架，通过将目标状态分解为多个正交成分并分别预测，提升了复杂系统中表示学习、预测和规划的稳定性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）使用单一目标嵌入和预测通路，导致在复杂系统中对主导信号过度分配容量，而对弱预测结构产生弱或冲突的梯度，限制模型性能。

Method: 该方法学习基矩阵将每个目标状态分解为多个成分，每个成分由独立的预测分支从共享上下文表示中估计；通过预测回归保持成分幅值，正交性目标避免方向重复，因子活动正则化维持投影目标变化，在线方差正则化防止编码器逐坐标坍缩；预测成分合成为完整潜在状态，支持解码、规划或自回归生成。

Result: 在受控视觉、单细胞转录组学、纵向健康记录、连续控制和分子动力学等实验中，验证了表示质量、预测、规划和长时程稳定性的提升。

Conclusion: 正交预测分解机制为复杂系统中学习结构化潜在状态提供了一种有效方案，并具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: World models construct latent states that support prediction, planning, and reasoning about an underlying system. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) offer a direct way to learn such states by predicting targets in representation space instead of reconstructing every detail of the observation. Standard JEPAs, however, organize all predictable content through one target embedding and one prediction pathway. In complex systems, this monolithic state can allocate redundant capacity to dominant signals while providing weak or conflicting gradients to less dominant predictive structure. We introduce \method, a latent world-modeling framework based on orthogonal predictive factorization. Learned basis matrices analyze each target state into multiple components, and a dedicated prediction branch estimates each component from a shared context representation. Predictive regression preserves the factor magnitudes required for state synthesis, an orthogonality objective discourages repeated directions, factor-activity regularization maintains variation in projected targets, and online variance regularization discourages coordinate-wise encoder collapse. Predicted components are synthesized into a complete latent state that can be used by a readout, decoder, planner, or autoregressive rollout. The same predictive-state mechanism applies when the target is temporally future, spatially hidden, or another partial observation of the same system. Experiments on controlled vision, single-cell transcriptomics, longitudinal health records, continuous control, and molecular dynamics evaluate representation quality, forecasting, planning, and long-horizon stability.

</details>


### [95] [Learning When to Think: Adaptive Reasoning for Test-Time Compute Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20256)
*Gijs Kassenaar,Zhao Yang,Vincent François-Lavet*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 通过让推理模型在回答首token自主选择推理模式（NoThink/Short/Long），在GRPO中学习自适应计算分配，在保持精度的同时大幅减少推理长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理模型使用固定token预算，导致简单问题过度计算、困难问题计算不足；希望模型自身学会按需分配推理长度，无需额外路由模块。

Method: 在GRPO中加入三模态选择作为首token，通过整形奖励使不同模式在不同长度上都有价值，并设置硬性token上限保证模式区分；训练1.5B蒸馏模型。

Result: 三个模式稳定出现未坍缩；短模式精度超过长模式，说明按难度排序；在MATH500上精度接近（0.782 vs 0.796），平均长度降低41%；在GSM8K上节省76%token且精度更高。

Conclusion: 模型能自适应选择推理长度，以更低计算开销保持甚至提升性能，并泛化至其他基准。

Abstract: Reasoning language models trained with reinforcement learning typically operate under a fixed token budget rather than an explicitly adaptive one, which can lead to over-computation on easy problems and insufficient computation on difficult ones. We study whether a model can learn to allocate its own reasoning effort by choosing, as the first token of its response, one of three modes: \textsc{NoThink} (answer as quickly as possible), \textsc{Short} (brief reasoning), or \textsc{Long} (extended reasoning). The choice is learned inside Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with no separate router, through a shaped reward that makes each mode worthwhile at a different response length, together with hard per-mode token caps that keep the modes distinct. On a 1.5B distilled model trained on MATH, the three modes emerge without collapsing to a single choice, and the brief modes end up more accurate than \textsc{Long}, which shows that the router sorts problems by difficulty rather than at random. Averaged over three seeds, the resulting policy stays close to the base model's accuracy on the held-out MATH500 ($0.782$ vs.\ $0.796$) while cutting the mean response length from $4{,}796$ to $2{,}811$ tokens (a $41\%$ reduction). Interestingly, it also transfers to other benchmarks without retraining, with the largest savings where problems are easier, with for instance 76\% token reduction on GSM8K and at higher accuracy than the baselines at similar response length. In short, we build a reasoning model that adaptively chooses how much to reason for each problem.

</details>


### [96] [Break It Down, Pass It On: Cross-Task Skill Transfer in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20274)
*Yiyang Feng,Biddut Sarker Bijoy,Niranjan Balasubramanian,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验系统比较了技能归纳方式（任务级 vs 子任务级、文本 vs 代码）对LLM智能体技能迁移效果的影响，发现子任务级和文本技能迁移更好，并提出结合特异性和抽象性的技能效用分数作为无需执行任务的迁移性能诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体可从完成任务中归纳技能并复用，但技能迁移可能不可靠甚至有害，而决定迁移可靠性的因素尚不明确。现有方法在技能归纳维度上存在分歧，缺乏系统对照研究。

Method: 沿两个轴进行受控对比实验：任务级vs子任务级技能归纳，以及文本vs代码技能格式；分析技能的特异性（贴近真实任务程度）和抽象性（相关任务分布均匀度），并设计结合两者的技能效用分数。

Result: 任务级技能多数情况下使智能体低于无记忆基线，子任务级技能平均提升表现；文本技能迁移优于代码技能。特异性或抽象性单独不能预测任务成功，但二者结合（技能效用分数）与迁移成功率一致相关，且子任务级和文本技能得分更高。

Conclusion: 技能效用分数仅需技能和任务描述即可计算，无需任务执行，可作为技能记忆的实用诊断工具，指导技能归纳方式选择以提升迁移可靠性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can induce skills from completed tasks and reuse them later to grow more capable with experience. In practice, induced skills may transfer unreliably and can even harm the agent that retrieves them. When agent-induced skills transfer reliably across tasks remains an open question. We conduct a comprehensive and controlled study of how the way skills are induced shapes their transfer across tasks. Specifically, we compare task-level with subtask-level skill induction and text with code skill formats, the two axes along which existing methods differ. Task-level skills mostly reduce the agent's performance below its no-memory baseline while subtask-level skills raise it above on average, and text skills transfer better than code skills. To further understand our findings, we examine two complementary properties of the induced skills: specificity, which measures how closely a skill matches real tasks, and abstractness, which measures how evenly its relevance spreads across tasks. Neither property alone predicts task success, but their combined effect does, which we propose as a skill utility score. The score correlates consistently with task success when skills are transferred, and subtask-level and text skills score higher. Computing skill utility only needs the skills and task descriptions but not any task execution, so our score serves as a practical diagnostic of a skill memory before any new task runs.

</details>


### [97] [Phantom Gains: Auditing Self-Improvement Against a Measured Null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20290)
*Cheng Xu,Nan Yan,Liming Chen,M-Tahar Kechadi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文揭示语言模型能力评估中因差异测量噪声导致的七种测量失效，提出基于基线复制的逐问题精确检验与错误发现率控制的审计框架，证明自训练未改善问题获取而外部蒸馏有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型能力提升的评判标准已从平均准确率转向个体问题的得失，但差分两个噪声估计易受测量伪影干扰，导致报告结论被逆转。

Method: 在冻结控制管道上对Qwen3-8B进行三轮rank-32 LoRA自训练审计，识别七种测量失效；用逐问题精确检验对比混合基线，并施加错误发现率控制；通过阶梯式多臂设计匹配流、体积和评估，测试外部蒸馏与三种自训练。

Result: 外部蒸馏提升基础模型难以触及的问题，三种自训练无此效果；回归检验拒绝不对称性源于蒸馏总体增益更大（p<10^-8）；自训练以高于基线失真率破坏已解决问题；新检验在保留复现上无检测结果且对多重检验规则不敏感。

Conclusion: 转换级审计必须为每个统计量提供单独测得的零分布，可从多臂研究已有的基线复现中构建，无需新实验，但需足够数量的复现。所有报告发现需同步控制组验证，否则可能被测量伪影逆转。

Abstract: Whether a language model has improved itself is increasingly judged not by mean accuracy but by which individual problems it gains and loses. Tracking these transitions means differencing two noisy estimates, leaving them vulnerable to measurement artifacts. Auditing three rounds of rank-$32$ LoRA self-training on Qwen3-8B against a frozen control pushed through the identical pipeline, we identify seven measurement failures, each of which inverts a reported finding when its control is absent. Several are standard practice. A ledger built on a single greedy decode manufactures capability changes on an untrained model, largely an artifact of inference batching; the expansion statistic separating acquisition from sharpening assigns that same model a rate of $0.280$. The natural threshold repair does not survive replication: estimated across the frozen comparisons such a design already contains, its null stays non-zero. We replace it with a per-problem exact test against a pooled baseline under false-discovery-rate control, which detects nothing on any held-out replicate and is unchanged under the multiple-testing rule, error rate and pool size. Applied to a ladder of arms matched in stream, volume and evaluation, the audit finds that external distillation improves problems the base model rarely reaches while three forms of self-training do not; a regression rejects this asymmetry as a by-product of distillation's larger overall gain ($p < 10^{-8}$). On the far smaller set of problems the base model never reaches, the evidence is inconclusive, while self-training corrupts problems solved at baseline at rates well above the measured floor. Transition-level auditing therefore requires a separately measured null for every statistic it reports: nulls that cost no new experiments, built from baseline replicates a multi-arm study already owns, though not from as few as most possess.

</details>


### [98] [MidTool: Mid-training Data Synthesis for Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20314)
*Fengqing Jiang,Yite Wang,Boyi Liu,Zhaoyang Wang,Canwen Xu,Zhewei Yao,Radha Poovendran,Yuxiong He*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出MidTool，一个面向通用工具使用能力的中期训练语料构建流程，通过混合Web、PDF、代码与合成监督数据，显著提升模型在工具使用基准上的下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中期训练对LLM能力塑造至关重要，但现有研究集中于数学、科学和软件工程等智能体能力，对通用工具使用这一平行能力探索不足，且该能力不应完全依赖后期训练。

Method: 构建MidTool管线，整合大规模网页、PDF、代码数据及来自真实工具API、MCP技能和文档驱动工作流的合成监督，设计任务涵盖工具识别、上下文参数落地、工作流组合及信息缺失恢复；在Qwen3-4B/8B-Base上进行中期训练，随后采用SFT和RL进行后期训练。

Result: 在BFCL、tau2-Bench和MCP Universe基准上，MidTool-Mix在SFT和RL两种设置下均一致优于基线，且对4B和8B模型均有效。

Conclusion: 通用工具使用能力同其他重要LLM能力一样，受益于专门的中期训练，而非完全依赖后期训练阶段。

Abstract: Mid-training is increasingly recognized as a critical stage for shaping the capabilities of large language models. Recent work has shown that targeted mid-training can strengthen reasoning-intensive abilities such as math and science, and can also improve agentic capabilities in software-engineering settings. In this work, we study the parallel but less explored agentic capability: general tool use. We present MidTool, an open corpus construction pipeline for agentic tool-use mid-training that combines large-scale web, PDF, and code data with synthesized supervision from real-world tool APIs, MCP skills, and document-grounded workflows. MidTool is designed to teach models how to recognize tool affordances, ground arguments from context, compose tool call workflow, and recover from incomplete information. We mid-train Qwen3-4B-Base and Qwen3-8B-Base on MidTool-Mix, and then apply follow-up post-training with both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Compared with baselines, MidTool-Mix consistently improves downstream performance under both SFT and RL on BFCL, tau2-Bench, and MCP Universe. These results suggest that general tool use, like other important LLM capabilities, benefits from dedicated mid-training rather than being left entirely to post-training.

</details>


### [99] [Decoding silent reading from non-invasive EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20186)
*Ingo Marquardt,Anthilia Alchanat,Priyanka Jain*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本研究利用干电极EEG在快速序列视觉呈现下记录约24万次单词呈现，结合对比学习解码器，证明开放词汇的词汇级信息可从静默阅读中恢复，且性能随数据量对数线性增长未饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非侵入式解码内心独白面临数据收集难题：无法获取自发内心独白的脑活动配对数据，现有代理范式（提示重复和回顾性生成）采集慢、时间锁定差且依从性难验证，因此需寻找可扩展的代理任务。

Method: 以静默阅读为代理任务，单个密集采样参与者进行393轮（约49小时）19通道干电极EEG记录，单词以快速序列视觉呈现且每次随机化字体以解耦词汇与低级视觉特征。使用卷积编码器（可选加因果Transformer），通过CLIP式对比损失对齐短时EEG窗口与大型语言模型的单词隐藏状态嵌入，评估采用词分组top-10检索并与置换基线比较。

Result: 解码可靠高于机会水平，涵盖中频和低频词汇，性能随训练数据量对数线性增长且无饱和迹象；移除枕叶和后颞电极使词汇级增益减少约三分之一，但上下文追踪不变；控制分析区分了词汇解码、叙事上下文追踪及Transformer位置嵌入引入的非神经位置先验。

Conclusion: 开放词汇的词汇级信息可从静默阅读EEG中恢复，且当前解码性能受限于数据量而非算法饱和，提示增加数据可进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Non-invasive decoding of inner speech faces a fundamental data problem: a corpus pairing brain activity with a person's spontaneous inner monologue cannot be collected, and the available proxy paradigms (cued repetitive and retrospectively reported generative inner speech) are slow to acquire, poorly time-locked, and subject compliance is unverifiable. We therefore treat silent reading as a scalable proxy task and ask how much lexical and semantic information a contrastive decoder can extract from it. We report an open-vocabulary analysis of approximately 240,000 word presentations recorded from a single densely-sampled participant across 393 runs (ca. 49 h) of 19-channel dry-electrode EEG. Words from continuous narrative text were presented in rapid serial visual presentation, with typography randomised on every trial to partially decorrelate word identity from low-level visual form. A convolutional EEG encoder, optionally followed by a causal transformer, was trained with a CLIP-style contrastive objective to align short EEG windows with hidden-state embeddings of the presented word taken from a large language model. Decoding, evaluated as word-grouped top-10 retrieval against permutation baselines, was reliably above chance, extended to mid-frequency and rare words, and scaled log-linearly with training-data volume with no sign of saturation. Removing occipital and posterior-temporal electrodes reduced the word-level gain by roughly one third but left context tracking unchanged. Control analyses separate word-level decoding from narrative context tracking and from a non-neural positional prior introduced by the transformer's positional embedding. These results establish that open-vocabulary word-level information is recoverable from EEG during silent reading, and that decoding is data-limited rather than saturated.

</details>


### [100] [Pandora's AI Model Routing Box: Efficient Allocation with Costly Value Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20316)
*Adam Fisch,Shubhendu Trivedi,Fantine Huot,William W. Cohen,Michael Kaisers,Mirella Lapata,Kate Larson,Jacob Eisenstein*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出将异构AI系统路由问题形式化为Pandora's Box最优搜索问题，通过价值信息理论决定是否值得细化评估，在集中式和去中心化场景下分别设计Pandora's Router与Pandora's Bidder策略，实验验证其在保持路由质量的同时大幅减少昂贵评估调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构AI系统通过将查询路由给最合适专家可提升质量与效率，但专家价值估计存在精度与成本权衡：廉价估计器噪声大，昂贵估计器代价高，亟需一种能动态决策是否值得细化评估的机制。

Method: 将路由问题建模为带昂贵检测的Pandora's Box问题，在高斯信号假设下推导闭式价值信息表达式，据此设计集中式策略Pandora's Router；并扩展至去中心化场景Pandora's Bidder，让专家独立决定是否投资自评估后再接受报价。

Result: 在三个领域（多LLM基准、检索增强专家、可变推理长度LLM）中，Pandora's Router匹配穷举估计的路由质量，同时显著降低昂贵估计器调用频率；去中心化场景中价值信息推理在估计准确时提升分配效率，但在估计噪声大时可能损害他人利益。

Conclusion: 价值信息理论为异构AI路由提供了可扩展的优化框架，能自适应平衡评估成本与路由质量；去中心化场景需谨慎设计激励，避免噪声估计下的策略性投机行为。

Abstract: Heterogeneous AI systems composed of multiple models, architectures, harnesses, or inference-time settings can improve quality and efficiency by routing queries to the specialist who can answer most effectively at the lowest cost. Routing requires estimating each specialist's expected return, but this value estimation has a cost. Cheap estimators (e.g., embedding-based predictors) are fast but noisy, while accurate estimators (e.g., fine-tuned models with access to retrieval results or partial reasoning traces) are expensive. We formalize this tradeoff as an instance of Pandora's Box, the classical problem of optimal search with costly inspection. Under a Gaussian signal model, the resulting policies have closed-form value-of-information expressions that determine, for each specialist and input, whether refining the value estimate is worth its cost. We call the centralized policy Pandora's Router. We extend this to a decentralized setting, Pandora's Bidder, where specialists independently decide whether to invest in self-assessment before accepting an offered price to claim a query. Experiments across three domains---a standard multi-LLM benchmark, retrieval-augmented specialists, and LLMs with variable inference-time reasoning---show that Pandora's Router matches the routing quality of exhaustive estimation, while querying the expensive estimator far less often. In the decentralized setting, value-of-information reasoning improves allocative efficiency when competing estimates are accurate; when competing estimates are noisy, however, it can increase the strategic specialist's utility at the expense of others.

</details>


### [101] [DICS: Data-Informed Centroid Splitting for Decision Tree Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20258)
*MD Saifur Rahman Mazumder,Feng Yu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出基于聚类的数据知情质心分裂（DICS）框架，通过数据驱动先验生成紧凑候选分裂集，显著降低分类树训练计算开销，同时保持预测精度，并兼容随机森林和梯度提升模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树训练因逐节点穷举搜索候选分裂而计算成本高昂，尤其面对大规模高维数据时，需在保证精度前提下降低计算复杂度。

Method: 利用类别感知的聚类框架构建候选分裂集，将分裂搜索空间压缩为数据驱动的质心点，并提供理论保证说明在假设条件下不劣于穷举搜索。

Result: 在合成与基准数据集上，DICS在保持相近精度的同时大幅减少训练时间，实验覆盖决策树、随机森林及梯度提升模型。

Conclusion: 将数据知情先验引入分裂选择可实现可扩展的高效分类树学习，兼顾精度与速度，为大数据场景提供实用方案。

Abstract: Decision tree-based models are widely used in machine learning due to their interpretability and strong empirical performance. However, training decision trees can be computationally expensive, particularly for large and high-dimensional datasets, largely due to the exhaustive search over candidate splits at each node. To improve computational efficiency, we propose Data-Informed Centroid Splitting (DICS), a clustering-based framework that constructs a compact and informative set of candidate splits using data-driven priors. By incorporating class-aware structure, DICS significantly reduces the split search space for classification tasks while preserving predictive performance. We further provide theoretical analysis showing that under the stated assumptions, DICS does not degrade the performance of classification trees compared to exhaustive split search. DICS can be incorporated into classification trees, random forests, and gradient-boosting models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DICS achieves comparable accuracy while substantially reducing training time across synthetic and benchmark datasets, highlighting the benefit of integrating data-informed priors into split selection for scalable classification tree learning.

</details>


### [102] [AI4AI-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents in Algorithmic Design for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20318)
*Yizhe Chi,Wenyi Li,Deyao Hong,Xiaoqiu Wang,Mingju Gao,Kaisen Yang,Bingxiang He,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出AI4AI-Bench基准，首次隔离评估AI系统设计训练算法的能力，发现现有系统在该任务上表现极弱，平均仅达原算法与最优间差距的16.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）要求AI能改进训练算法本身，但现有基准无法独立测量此能力——它们混淆了数据收集、超参数调优与算法设计。

Method: 构造10个冻结研究仓库，覆盖10类训练算法家族；Agent在4小时内重写算法，随后从零重跑至多12小时，由隐藏评估器对比原算法打分。所有指标映射到同一标尺（0为无信息模型，0.1为仓库原算法，1.0为最优）。

Result: 6个系统29种配置下平均分0.166，最佳达0.250；多数提交未改变学习方式，少数改变者平均0.226 vs 未改变者0.126；更多推理努力将改变者比例从8%提升至64%，平均分从0.094升至0.196。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统在训练算法设计上能力极为有限，距RSI要求甚远；该基准及所有提交已开源，支持未来重复测量以追踪进展。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) asks whether an AI system can improve the process that produces AI systems, so that the next system inherits the improvement. That process is the training algorithm: a better objective or update rule improves the compute\mbox{-}capability exchange rate for every subsequent run, including the one that produces the next agent. Whether RSI is feasible therefore turns on whether an agent can design training algorithms. No benchmark isolates that ability: existing suites are won by collecting data or by tuning hyperparameters, and none tells a change to how a run is executed apart from a change to how the model learns. We present AI4AI\mbox{-}Bench, 10 frozen research repositories spanning 10 training algorithm families. In each task, an agent has 4 hours on one B300 to rewrite the training algorithm; its code is then rerun from scratch for up to 12 hours and scored by a fixed evaluator hidden from the agent, against the repository's original algorithm under the same procedure. Because the 10 metrics are incommensurable, every task is mapped onto one scale on which $0$ is an uninformative model, $0.1$ is the algorithm the repository ships, and $1.0$ is the task optimum. Across 29 configurations of 6 systems on all 10 tasks the mean score is $0.166$, and the best system reaches $0.250$: even the strongest closes under a fifth of the distance between the algorithm that was already there and the optimum. The submissions show where that distance went: most never change how the model learns at all, and the minority that do average $0.226$ against $0.126$ for the rest. More reasoning effort mostly buys the willingness to go there, taking that minority from $8\%$ of submissions to $64\%$ and the mean score from $0.094$ to $0.196$. We release the task suite, the evaluators and every scored submission, so that the measurement can be repeated as these systems change.

</details>


### [103] [Physical-Support Confidence Sets for Highly Coherent Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20295)
*Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种分辨率感知的物理支撑推断方法，联合考虑字典学习和信号表示的不确定性，通过交叉字典置信对应保留兼容解释，并开发主动端点括号化（AEB）算法在计算上安全地避免过度精确的物理报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字典学习后的稀疏追踪即使在物理解释不被校准数据支持时也可能给出精确的原子支撑，尤其在高度相干字典中，不同的校准兼容字典可能对同一支撑赋予不同物理意义，导致物理推断不可靠。

Method: 构建交叉字典置信对应，同时保留校准兼容字典和部署兼容稀疏表示，并将存留解释投影到物理支撑空间；推导极小极大物理分辨率界限；提出AEB自适应有限库过程，仅评估可能影响物理报告的候选，其余安全粗化或弃权。

Result: 对局部相干原子类（分离尺度s），部署数据解决相干块解释后，极小极大物理分辨率δ_opt(N,s)≍min{s,1/(√N s^2)}，相对分辨率受方向信息尺度Ns^6控制；AEB在四区域合成实验中避免了点值代入选择器的物理过度精确，且候选评估更少。

Conclusion: 物理推断需显式建模字典与表示的双重不确定性，AEB提供了一种计算高效的策略，在避免不支持细化的同时保持可靠性，部署复制仅在方向变化无法被活动系数吸收时改善物理定位。

Abstract: Sparse pursuit after dictionary learning can yield a precise atom support even when its physical interpretation is not justified by the calibration data, especially for highly coherent dictionaries where alternative calibration-compatible dictionaries may assign different physical meanings to the same selected support. We develop resolution-aware physical-support inference that jointly accounts for uncertainty in the learned dictionary and in the representation of a deployment signal. Our cross-dictionary confidence correspondence retains calibration-compatible dictionaries and deployment-compatible sparse representations, then projects the surviving explanations onto physical-support space. For local coherent-atom classes with separation scale s, once the deployment data resolve the coherent-block explanation and its atom support, the minimax physical resolution from N calibration signals satisfies $δ_{\mathrm{opt}}(N,s)\asymp\min\{s,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}s^2}\}$, with relative resolution governed by the orientation-information scale $Ns^6$. Deployment replication improves physical localization only when orientation changes cannot be absorbed by adjusting the active coefficients. For computation, we introduce active endpoint bracketing (AEB), an adaptive finite-bank procedure that evaluates only candidates that can still affect the physical report and otherwise safely coarsens or abstains. Finite-bank experiments, including a four-region synthetic application, show that a point-valued plug-in selector can be physically overprecise, whereas AEB avoids unsupported refinement with fewer candidate evaluations.

</details>


<div id='Sec'></div>

# Sec [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Incident-Data Robustness Analysis of the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications (2026): How a Community-Expert Ranking Holds Up Against a Large-Scale LLM Incident Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19266)
*Kyriakos "Rock" Lambros,Steve Wilson*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 基于7,714个LLM安全事件构建语料库，用贝叶斯模型修正分类误差后，发现专家排名与实际事件排名一致性弱（κ≈0.20），但事件数据无法推翻专家共识，最终采用0.75专家+0.25数据的加权融合方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OWASP Top 10是专家共识排名，但缺乏与真实事件记录的对照验证。本文旨在检验专家排名是否与实际安全事件分布一致，并探索数据驱动修正的可能性。

Method: 构建包含CVE、GHSA、OSV、AIAAIC来源的7,714个事件快照，标注6,639条至20类安全分类；使用贝叶斯测量误差模型校正分类器精度和召回率偏差；以固定权重（0.75专家+0.25数据）融合排名；通过预注册测试比较四个前沿分类器性能。

Result: 专家排名与事件排名一致性弱（Cohen's κ≈0.20，90%置信区间跨零）；四个前沿分类器均未超过简单基线（平衡准确率0.863）；基线排序与保留真值高度相关（Spearman ρ=0.918）。

Conclusion: 专家研判虽与事件数据存在分歧，但数据修正仅作辅助（权重0.25），无法推翻专家共识。该研究为探索性分析，不替代官方OWASP流程。

Abstract: The OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications ranks the risks that a community of security practitioners judges most important. We ask a narrower question: checked against the record of real incidents, does that expert ranking agree with the data? We assembled a large-scale corpus of LLM-security incidents (7,714 snapshotted and 6,639 labeled against the 20-entry taxonomy) drawn from CVE, GHSA, OSV, and AIAAIC, and derived an incident-based ranking with a Bayesian measurement-error model that corrects each category's count for classifier precision and recall. The 2026 candidate list blends the two signals at fixed weights, 0.75 on the expert vote and 0.25 on the data, so the corpus corrects the consensus without overturning it. The agreement between the two rankings is weak: Cohen's $κ\approx 0.20$, with a 90% interval that crosses zero. The expert ranking is nonetheless robust. A pre-registered bake-off of four frontier classifiers returns no winner. None beats the incidence floor's balanced accuracy of 0.863. A ground-truth check leaves the floor's ordering (Spearman $ρ= 0.918$ against held-out truth) in place. This is an exploratory analysis by two working-group members, not the official OWASP release, and it does not supersede the official list or process.

</details>


### [105] [ABEAT: Efficient and Anonymous Encryption for ABE-based Dynamic Group Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19302)
*Hongmiao Yu,Silas Richelson,Jiachen Chen,K. K. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出ABEAT，一个基于匿名KP-ABE和图形命名空间的动态组通信系统，在保持接收者匿名性的同时，将非接收者的解密开销降低90倍以上，并优于无匿名性的FABEO方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于属性加密（ABE）的组通信方案缺乏接收者匿名性，且非目标接收者解密开销过高，无法满足应急响应等动态场景下高效、灵活且保护隐私的组通信需求。

Method: 设计匿名密钥策略属性加密（anonymous KP-ABE）方案，在密文中隐藏明确属性；结合图形化命名空间支持动态组管理；引入快速接收者验证机制以显著降低非接收者的解密计算量。

Result: 与当前最先进的隐向量加密（HVE）相比，非接收者解密开销降低超过90倍；相比无匿名性的FABEO，开销再降低40%。

Conclusion: ABEAT在保证接收者匿名性和动态组通信灵活性的同时，大幅提升效率，为机密组通信提供了一种实用且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Confidential communication among a dynamic group of participants that ensures flexible and efficient many-to- many communication is highly desired capability. We leverage attribute-based encryption (ABE) for confidential group communication and enhance it by a graph-based namespace to create an efficient framework that allows groups to be formed and changed dynamically. In this paper, we focus on the important additional need to maintain the anonymity of recipients of a message, when using ABE for group communication for a variety of usage scenarios (e.g., emergency response).
  We propose ABEAT, an efficient and anonymous dynamic group communication system that also minimizes overhead on receivers who are not the intended recipients of a message. In ABEAT, we propose a new anonymous KP-ABE approach to maintain recipient anonymity. ABEAT hides the clear attribute in the ciphertext of KP-ABE, and also prevents several attacks that seek to break anonymity. ABEAT provides fast recipient verification, dramatically lowering the decryption overhead for non-recipients by more than a factor of 90 versus the current state of the art such as hidden vector encryption (HVE). In fact, it is even 40% less than FABEO, which offers no anonymity.

</details>


### [106] [Redactable blockchains and polynomial equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19401)
*Alexander Demin,Alexey Ovchinnikov,Vladimir Shpilrain*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出了新的工具，用于构建具有后量子安全性的可编辑认证数据结构，其安全性基于求解多变元多项式方程（组）的困难性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可编辑认证数据结构在后量子时代面临安全性挑战，需要构造能抵御量子攻击的新方案。

Method: 通过设计一种新的单向函数，其求逆等价于求解多变元多项式方程组，并合理选择参数以确保无已知高效量子算法。

Result: 成功构建了后量子安全的可编辑认证数据结构，其安全性归约到多变元多项式方程求解的困难性。

Conclusion: 该工作为后量子可编辑认证数据结构提供了新的构造工具，拓展了量子安全密码学在数据认证领域的应用。

Abstract: We develop new tools for constructing redactable authenticated data structures with post-quantum security. In our construction, inverting the proposed one-way function means solving a polynomial equation (or a system of polynomial equations) in more than one variable. This is presently considered quantum-safe, i.e., there is no known quantum algorithm that could solve this problem efficiently if parameters are chosen wisely.

</details>


### [107] [Robust Metaheuristics under Uncertainty for Berth Allocation and Quay Crane Assignment: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19214)
*Yang Li,Peilan Xu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文是首篇聚焦于不确定性下鲁棒种群元启发式算法求解泊位分配与岸桥调度问题（BACAP）的综述，系统梳理了不确定性来源、方法机制、基准测试及开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒元启发式研究在不确定性表示、鲁棒性准则、搜索机制和实验协议上碎片化，缺乏系统综述，导致方法对比和落地应用困难。

Method: 从机制导向视角组织方法，涵盖解表示与解码、鲁棒评估与选择、鲁棒性引导的搜索动态、可行性保持与恢复，并构建了不确定BACAP基准套件。

Result: 提供了基准测试套件和结合代表性元启发式与不同鲁棒策略的基线实验结果，支撑受控实证比较。

Conclusion: 指出了基准扩展、鲁棒感知搜索设计、时间自适应鲁棒性及非平稳不确定性等未来研究方向。

Abstract: The berth allocation and quay crane assignment problem (BACAP) is a representative port-terminal scheduling problem in maritime transportation and freight logistics, where vessel arrivals, berth positions, service durations, and quay?crane availability are tightly coupled. Under uncertainties such as arrival deviations, handling-time fluctuations, and resource disruptions, schedules optimized under nominal assumptions may become fragile during execution, motivating the study of robust metaheuristic optimization for BACAP in port-terminal operations. Although population-based metaheuristics have been widely used for BACAP and related port-scheduling problems, existing studies remain fragmented in their uncertainty repre?sentations, robustness criteria, search mechanisms, and empir?ical evaluation protocols. To the best of our knowledge, this paper provides the first focused review dedicated to robust population-based metaheuristics for BACAP under uncertainty. We first summarize uncertainty sources and information repre?sentations in BACAP, and then organize existing methods from a mechanism-oriented perspective, covering solution representation and decoding, robust evaluation and selection, robustness-guided search dynamics, and feasibility preservation and recovery. We further present a benchmark suite for uncertain BACAP to support controlled empirical comparison and report illustrative baseline results by combining representative metaheuristics with different robustness strategies. Finally, we identify open chal?lenges related to benchmark extension, robustness-aware search design, time-adaptive robustness, and non-stationary uncertainty.

</details>


### [108] [AEGIS: Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield - Triple-Channel Gradient Masking for Privacy-Preserving Federated LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19534)
*Ye Tao,Hong Shen,Hui Tian,Xin Wang,Can Wang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出AEGIS防御方法，通过三项反向传播操作关闭Transformer梯度中的三个隐私泄露通道，在不改变架构的情况下将令牌恢复率降至近零，同时保持或提升模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度反演攻击利用Transformer梯度结构中的三个通道泄露私有训练文本，现有防御只针对单个通道且会损害效用或留下结构信号，亟需统一防御。

Method: AEGIS冻结注意力投影参数消除通道1，向嵌入梯度注入校准噪声破坏通道2的令牌存在信号，向MLP扩展梯度注入逐块噪声掩蔽通道3；同一掩蔽梯度同时用于本地优化和服务端导出，不保留干净信号。

Result: 在11个模型和6个数据集上，针对分析和优化两类攻击，令牌恢复率降至近零，同时保持或提升模型效用，并对具有完全机制知识的自适应对手有效。

Conclusion: AEGIS是一种轻量、无架构改动且可证明有效的防御方案，正式保证通道1和2，实证验证通道3，为联邦学习中的隐私保护提供了坚实解决方案。

Abstract: Gradient inversion attacks recover private training text from gradients shared in federated learning, posing a serious threat to collaborative model training. Through our analysis of transformer gradient structure, we identify three channels through which private token information leaks: the attention output projection gradient exposes a low-rank subspace that encodes input embeddings (Channel 1), the embedding gradient's row-norm sparsity directly reveals which tokens are present (Channel 2), and the MLP expansion gradient carries a recoverable subspace signal analogous to Channel 1 (Channel 3). State-of-the-art attacks exploit these channels analytically to achieve near-exact token recovery in seconds. Existing defences address at most one channel and either degrade model utility or leave the remaining structural signals intact.
  We introduce AEGIS (Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield), a lightweight defence that closes all three analytical channels with three backward-path operations requiring no architectural changes: freezing attention projection parameters eliminates Channel 1 by construction, calibrated noise injection into the embedding gradient destroys Channel 2's token-presence signal, and analogous per-block noise injection into the MLP expansion gradient masks Channel 3. The same masked gradient drives both the local optimiser step and the server export, so no clean signal is retained on either side.
  Evaluated across 11 models and six datasets, AEGIS reduces token recovery rates to near zero against a range of gradient inversion attacks, both analytical and optimisation-based, while preserving or improving model utility. We provide formal guarantees for Channels 1 and 2 and validate the full defence empirically against adaptive adversaries with complete knowledge of the mechanism.

</details>


### [109] [Enhancing Privacy in Federated Learning via Dual Obfuscation of Gradients and Training Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19650)
*Yuki Itabashi,Hiroto Sawada,Mare Hirose,Shoko Imaizumi,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种双混淆方法，结合随机二进制权重梯度稀疏化与图像加密，在联邦学习中抵御图像重建攻击，在不额外降低分类性能的前提下减少攻击者恢复的视觉信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽将数据保留在本地，但共享模型更新仍可被用于重建训练图像，现有防御措施不足，需要更有效的隐私保护方法。

Method: 联合两种互补技术：一是基于随机二进制权重的鲁棒性增强，将部分梯度元素随机置零以减少原始梯度信息；二是图像加密，降低重建图像的视觉可解释性，且支持每客户端每图像独立密钥，无需显式共享密钥。

Result: 在Vision Transformer图像分类任务上，所提方法在评估设置下减少了APRIL攻击恢复的视觉信息，且未造成超出图像加密本身的分类性能额外下降。

Conclusion: 虽然该方法不提供绝对安全保证，但结果表明结合梯度修改与图像加密在隐私增强联邦学习中具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training while keeping data locally at each client; however, recent studies have shown that training data can be reconstructed from shared model updates. To address this issue, this paper proposes a dual obfuscation method that enhances robustness against image restoration attacks by jointly obfuscating updated information and training images. The proposed method combines a robustness enhancement technique based on random binary weights, which randomly sets a portion of gradient elements to zero, with an image encryption technique. These techniques provide complementary protection by reducing the amount of original gradient information available to an attacker and the visual interpretability of reconstructed images, respectively. Furthermore, the image encryption technique allows independent keys to be used for each client and each image, avoiding explicit key sharing. Experimental results on an image classification task using a Vision Transformer (ViT) show that the proposed method reduces the visual information recovered by Attention Privacy Leakage (APRIL) under the evaluated settings without causing additional degradation in classification performance beyond that caused by image encryption. Although the proposed combination does not provide an absolute security guarantee, the results demonstrate the potential benefit of combining gradient modification and image encryption for privacy-enhanced federated learning.

</details>


### [110] [Bounded Sovereignty and the Control Tax: Pricing AI Oversight When the Deployer Does Not Own the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19216)
*Zhen Wen Lim*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出"有界主权"概念，系统化分析受监管组织通过API部署前沿模型时，因无法掌控模型、基础设施等层级而面临的AI控制协议执行限制，并构建四层访问类型学与协议需求矩阵，通过合成实验量化访问缺失带来的"主权折扣成本"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制研究假设部署者能完全掌控模型及管线，但这在受监管组织通过API使用前沿模型时往往不成立。部署者只能控制业务流程，无法控制权重、基础设施、内部日志等，导致控制协议实际不可执行。

Method: 提出四层访问类型学（数据、模型、基础设施、交互），构建协议-层级需求矩阵；引入"主权折扣成本"概念；开展135万条合成案例的访问消融实验，并辅以匿名国家支付基础设施场景进行构念效度解读。

Result: 完整日志提升诊断能力；执行前网关支持干预；追踪访问与模型版本控制增强事后解释；限制系统范围可提升安全性但降低实用性。合成实验仅为构念效度练习，非真实支付系统证据。

Conclusion: 控制协议应明确声明其访问假设，否则在受监管API部署场景下可能失效。有界主权框架为评估和设计现实可行的安全控制方案提供了结构化基础。

Abstract: AI control research asks how to deploy models safely even when they may be misaligned, but many control protocols assume that the deployer can instrument the model and its surrounding pipeline. That assumption often fails for regulated organisations using frontier models through APIs or managed endpoints, where the deployer may control the business process but not the model weights, serving infrastructure, internal traces, update process, or full interaction logs. This paper introduces bounded sovereignty: partial technical and contractual access across the data, model, infrastructure, and interaction layers of the AI stack. It argues that these access conditions determine which control protocols can be executed in practice. The paper contributes a four-layer access typology, a protocol-by-layer requirements matrix, and the concept of sovereignty discount cost: the part of the control tax spent substituting for missing access through contracts, architecture, audit, vendor assurance, residual risk, or reduced system scope. It also reports a synthetic access-ablation experiment over 1.35 million synthetic case simulations and interprets the findings through an anonymised national-payments-infrastructure scenario. The experiment is not real-world payment-system evidence; it is a construct-validity exercise. The results show that complete logs improve diagnosis, a pre-execution gateway enables intervention, trace access and model-version control strengthen post-incident explanation, and scope restriction can improve safety while reducing usefulness. Control protocols proposed as general safety solutions should therefore state their access assumptions explicitly.

</details>


### [111] [TGL-APT: Temporal Graph Learning with Graph Distillation for Efficient APT Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19750)
*Jing Chen,Ayong Ye,Yuanhuang Liu,Yuexin Zhang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出TGL-APT框架，利用信息瓶颈节点进行图蒸馏、自适应时序图学习和跨时空指纹对齐，在DARPA E3数据集上以更低开销实现优于KAIROS的APT检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APT攻击的隐蔽多阶段特性使传统检测失效，而全图学习的计算成本高且难以关联长序列攻击；攻击相关信息分布不均，主要由结构关键或行为独特的实体（信息瓶颈节点）介导。

Method: 结合三个组件：信息瓶颈引导的图蒸馏（抑制冗余但保持结构完整性和因果可达性）、自适应时序图学习（动态更新核心节点集）、跨时空攻击指纹对齐（关联不同实体和时间窗口的碎片活动），最后通过因果扩展和阶段特征重构攻击过程。

Result: 在三个DARPA E3数据集上F1分数为95.7%、90.9%、88.9%，相比KAIROS降低了约39%训练时间、33%检测延迟和22%内存使用。

Conclusion: TGL-APT在检测性能、计算效率和调查能力之间取得了有效平衡，适用于基于溯源图的APT分析。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threat (APT) attacks pose a critical challenge to modern systems, as their stealthy, multi-stage nature renders conventional detection methods ineffective. While provenance graphs provide rich behavioral context for attack investigation, attack-relevant evidence is often sparse and embedded in large volumes of routine system activity, making full-graph learning both computationally expensive and difficult to correlate over long attack sequences. We present TGL-APT, an adaptive investigation framework built on the observation that attack-relevant information is non-uniformly distributed and often mediated by structurally influential or behaviorally distinctive entities, which we characterize as information-bottleneck nodes. TGL-APT combines three complementary components: (1) information-bottleneck-guided graph distillation that suppresses provenance redundancy while bounding structural distortion and preserving causal reachability; (2) adaptive temporal graph learning that continuously refines the core node set as node relevance evolves; and (3) cross-spatiotemporal attack fingerprint alignment that associates fragmented suspicious activities across different entities and time windows. Finally, causal expansion and stage characterization reconstruct coherent attack processes for investigation. Experiments on three DARPA E3 datasets show F1-scores of 95.7%, 90.9%, and 88.9%, while reducing training time, detection latency, and memory usage by approximately 39%, 33%, and 22%, respectively, compared with KAIROS. These results demonstrate that TGL-APT effectively balances detection performance, computational efficiency, and investigation capability for provenance-based APT analysis.

</details>


### [112] [Survival of~the~Stealthiest: Evolving Low-Entropy Ransomware via~Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19821)
*Efrat Levenberg,Kristina Sviazhina,Ayelet Butman,Pierre Parrend,Harel Berger*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 将勒索软件执行框架化为基于搜索的软件工程优化问题，利用遗传算法在系统活动统计偏差的硬约束下优化数据加密，生成的攻击模式可逃避行为监控器的指纹识别，表明搜索方法可生成规避性恶意软件，对自动化软件防御构成新兴挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统勒索软件依赖大规模加密过程，易被现代防御系统即时检测；而现代威胁存在持久性缺口，即攻击需在数小时而非数分钟内保持隐蔽，因此需要一种新方法在加密与隐蔽之间寻求平衡。

Method: 采用遗传算法（GA）作为搜索优化手段，将数据加密过程建模为SBSE优化问题，并以系统活动基线统计偏差作为硬性约束条件，迭代进化攻击模式以最小化可检测性。

Result: 实验表明，进化出的攻击模式能够有效规避基于指纹识别技术的行为监控器，证明在约束条件下可实现隐蔽且有效的加密操作。

Conclusion: 搜索-based方法为生成规避性恶意软件提供了强大框架，揭示了自动化软件防御面临的新兴挑战，暗示未来防御需应对此类优化驱动的攻击策略。

Abstract: Traditional ransomware deployment often relies on massive encryption procedure, triggering immediate detection by modern defense systems. This work introduces a paradigm shift in cryptographic attacks by framing ransomware execution as a Search-Based Software Engineering (SBSE) optimization problem. This approach addresses the persistence gap observed in modern threats, where attacks aim to remain undercover for hours rather than minutes. Using a Genetic Algorithm (GA), we optimize data encryption under a hard constraint on the statistical deviation from baseline system activity. We demonstrate that our evolved attack patterns can evade behavioral monitors under fingerprinting techniques. Our results suggest that search-based methods provide a powerful framework for generating evasive malware, highlighting an emerging challenge for automated software defense.

</details>


### [113] [Air Traffic Control Using Large Language Models: Prompt Engineering, Architecture, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19299)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Matthias Casanova,Jordan Kam,Alex Zongo,Peng Wei,Torsten Darrell,Alexandre Bayen*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本研究实验评估了LLM在航空交通管制对话中生成逼真指令的能力，发现提供范例可提升相似度，但过度约束的提示反而导致错误累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATC通信是安全关键的对话，目前仍高度依赖人工，尽管空管其他部分已半自动化，因此探索LLM辅助ATC的可行性及当前限制。

Method: 基于人工转录的旧金山Bay Tour航线真实航班构建基准P0，通过飞行员参与设计五种约束递增的提示结构（P1-P5），在状态化多轮管线中测试9种开源/闭源LLM，变化提示、上下文范例以及历史条件方式，用词汇、结构、语义相似度及GPT-5.5评判并验证人类专家标注。

Result: 提供工作范例提升相似度；约束最强的提示性能最差，因自生成错误在对话中累积，注入正确历史可修复。

Conclusion: 研究勾勒出LLM辅助ATC的可行路径及当前局限，建议轻量提示和正确历史注入是关键。

Abstract: Air traffic control (ATC) communication is a safety-critical dialogue that remains largely human-driven even as other parts of air traffic management have been semi-automated. In this article, we experimentally evaluate whether large language models (LLMs) can generate operationally realistic ATC transmissions. An experimental general-aviation flight flying over the San Francisco "Bay Tour" route is hand-transcribed and used as ground truth (P0). Through a pilot-in-the-loop process we design five prompt structures (P1-P5) of increasing constraint and embed them in a stateful multi-turn pipeline, where the model plays ATC to a fixed pilot transcript while conditioning on the accumulating dialogue history. Across nine open- and closed-source LLMs we vary the prompt, the presence of a worked transcript from a different experimental flight as an in-context example, and whether the model conditions on its own prior replies or on injected ground-truth history. Turns are scored with lexical, structural, and semantic similarity metrics and by an LLM-as-judge (GPT-5.5) validated against human expert annotation. Supplying a worked example improves similarity, but tightening the prompt does not: the lightest prompts perform best and the most heavily scripted one collapses as its own errors accumulate through the dialogue, which injecting correct history repairs. These results outline a concrete path and its current limits toward LLM-assisted ATC.

</details>


### [114] [MaliciousSkillBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Malicious Agent Skill Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19901)
*Yue Wang,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Ying Zhang,Yuekang Li,Zhenyu Chen,Leo Zhang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出恶意技能检测基准MaliciousSkillBench，整合13个数据源并构建9740个技能样本，发现现有检测器在跨源评估中性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恶意技能数据集碎片化且重复，缺乏统一评估基准，阻碍了可靠检测方法的发展。

Method: 整合多源数据，进行归一化去重和结构聚类，协调11类攻击标签，并评估三种学习型检测器与三种现成扫描器。

Result: 学习型检测器随机F1达0.882-0.932但源分离降至0.653-0.665；最强TF-IDF SVM在源分离下F1为0.665，恶意召回95.6%但误报率62.4%。

Conclusion: 可靠检测需更广的跨源覆盖和联合评估攻击检测与良性误报，现有方法在跨源场景下均不充分。

Abstract: Agent Skills extend LLM agents with reusable instruction packages that may also include scripts, resources, and service configuration. This creates a direct distribution channel for malicious behavior, yet existing malicious-Skill datasets are fragmented across sources, artifact formats, evidence regimes, and benign coverage; duplicated and structurally related content further complicates direct aggregation and evaluation. We present MaliciousSkillBench, a comprehensive benchmark for malicious Agent Skill detection. We consolidate 13 public sources, 11 of which contribute Core malicious artifacts, and reduce 8,414 raw malicious records to 7,539 normalized-unique identities in 4,588 operational structural families. After conservative cross-label conflict exclusion, the primary benchmark contains 9,740 Skills: 7,505 malicious and 2,235 benign. To characterize its coverage, we harmonize 11 attack categories for 4,983 malicious identities with supported source-native mappings and find substantial differences in threat composition across sources. We then evaluate three learned text detectors and three off-the-shelf Skill scanners. Learned detectors achieve 0.882-0.932 Random Macro-F1 but only 0.653-0.665 under Source-Disjoint evaluation; the strongest word TF-IDF SVM scores 0.932/0.916/0.665 on Random/structural-disjoint/Source-Disjoint while retaining 95.6% malicious recall but producing 62.4% benign FPR on held-out sources. Off-the-shelf scanners occupy different but also unsatisfactory operating regimes, reducing false positives only at the cost of sharply lower malicious recall. Together, these results show that reliable malicious-Skill detection requires both broader cross-source benchmark coverage and evaluation that jointly measures attack detection and benign over-flagging.

</details>


### [115] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于模板的神经网络鲁棒性验证加速技术的有效性，提出联合稳定神经元指标解释性能差异，并设计FastCert方法自动分配模板以提升加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模板复用加速技术在部分场景有效，但其效果随网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法变化，缺乏系统性分析，且可能在某些情况下完全无效。

Method: 通过大规模实验分析模板包含率的变化，提出联合稳定神经元新指标解释差异；设计FastCert方法，自动跨层分配模板，并在无加速潜力时完全放弃模板。

Result: 在基于覆盖设计的L0验证任务中，FastCert相比现有模板复用技术实现平均1.13倍加速。

Conclusion: 模板加速效果高度依赖具体场景，FastCert通过自适应模板分配有效提升性能，但需根据网络特性决定是否使用模板。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>


### [116] [Securing Filesystems for Confidential Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19924)
*Dimitra Giantsidi,Antoine Delignat-Lavaud,Cédric Fournet,Jinnan Guo,Heidi Howard,Tianjiao Huang,Kapil Vaswani,Stavros Volos*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: ShieldFS是一个兼容POSIX的文件系统，在不修改应用的前提下，为机密计算环境中的持久存储提供端到端的完整性和新鲜性保障，能抵御恶意云提供商的回滚、重放、分叉和篡改攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算虽能保护TEE内的应用，但存储仍易受攻击，现有方案要么依赖可信存储、要么开销高、要么要求应用修改，缺乏兼顾安全与性能的通用解决方案。

Method: ShieldFS在TEE内维护简洁的密码学承诺并复制到轻量可信注册表，使用哈希链和嵌入式Merkle树认证写前日志和存储池，结合事务与写时复制原子更新状态和承诺，读取时验证承诺以检测攻击。实现上扩展ZFS形成ShieldZFS。

Result: 基准测试和真实负载表明，ShieldZFS在提供强完整性、新鲜性保证的同时，性能与现有先进文件系统相当。

Conclusion: ShieldFS/ShieldZFS证明在不可信I/O栈下实现高效、免应用修改的持久存储完整性保护是可行的，为机密计算存储安全提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Confidential computing protects applications inside Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), but it leaves storage vulnerable. Even with disk encryption, a malicious cloud provider can roll back, replay, fork, or tamper with disk state, breaking the integrity and freshness guarantees required by stateful applications. Existing solutions either assume trusted storage, incur high overheads, or push integrity logic into applications. We present ShieldFS, a POSIX-compliant filesystem that provides end-to-end integrity and freshness for persistent storage in the confidential-computing threat model without requiring application changes. ShieldFS represents permissible filesystem states using succinct cryptographic commitments, maintained inside TEEs and replicated in a lightweight trusted registry. On-disk data structures, including a write-ahead log and a storage pool, are authenticated using hash chains and an embedded Merkle tree. ShieldFS utilizes transactions and copy-on-write to update persistent filesystem state and commitments atomically. The commitments are verified during reads, ensuring that rollback, replay, and equivocation attacks are detected even when the entire I/O stack is untrusted. We implement the design by extending ZFS, yielding ShieldZFS. Evaluation with standard filesystem benchmarks and real-world workloads shows that ShieldZFS provides strong integrity and freshness guarantees with performance comparable to state-of-the-art filesystems.

</details>


### [117] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出EventTime框架，结合长短期市场上下文与事件元数据，通过动态对比学习预测网络安全事件披露后的短期异常损失，在真实数据集SECURE上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全事件等外生冲击罕见且异质，传统时序模型依赖内生规律难以应对；事件影响预测需在稀疏监督和噪声中估计短期损失，而非重建完整轨迹。

Method: 多分辨率框架EventTime，融合长周期市场背景、短周期事件前动态及事件属性；事件融合模块耦合时间表示与属性；动态对比学习构造事件与时序感知的正负样本。

Result: 在SECURE数据集上，EventTime在预测事件后财务损失方面一致优于SOTA时序和事件感知基线，且对元数据缺失更鲁棒，表示更具事件敏感性。

Conclusion: 验证了事件条件影响预测的可行性与有效性，提供可解释的短期市场冲击估计，为网络安全披露的金融影响分析提供新工具。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses. Further analyses demonstrate more event-sensitive representations, greater robustness to incomplete metadata, and more interpretable estimates of short-term market impact following cybersecurity disclosures.

</details>


### [118] [From Noise to Signal: Improving Security Log Anomaly Detection Using LLMs with Endpoint-Specific Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19938)
*Christopher Henshaw,Gour Karmakar*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出标准化指令LLM分类框架检测异常认证行为，在专用测试床数据集上，Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct综合性能最佳，显著优于Wazuh和OpenSearch，尤其对边界案例检测优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规则（如Wazuh）和统计（如OpenSearch）的日志异常检测方法依赖预定义规则或历史模式，能力受限；LLM方法受提示构造、噪声数据及缺乏端点特定认证行为数据集影响。需开发标准化LLM框架并构建端侧专属数据以提升检测效果。

Method: 构建受控网络安全测试床生成端点特定认证数据，包含正常、边界和异常三类场景；设计标准化指令模板，评估三种指令微调LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct、Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct、GPT-OSS 20B），并与Wazuh规则检测及OpenSearch异常检测在统一严重性框架下对比。

Result: Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct取得最佳端到端性能：准确率89.3%、召回率88.2%、F1分数91.8%、假阴性率11.8%；边界异常检测率80%。Wazuh准确率52.0%、假阴性率68.6%；OpenSearch准确率49.3%、假阴性率74.5%。Qwen延迟最低且结构化响应有效性100%，GPT-OSS在有效响应时分类性能强。

Conclusion: 标准化指令LLM框架在认证异常检测上显著优于传统规则和统计方法，尤其在识别边界案例方面；不同LLM在性能、延迟和响应有效性上各有权衡，Meta Llama综合最优，Qwen适合低延迟场景。

Abstract: Existing approaches to anomalous behaviour log detection, such as Wazuh rely primarily on predefined detection rules, while statistical anomaly detection approaches such as OpenSearch identify deviations from previously observed behavioural patterns. Recent research has investigated LLMs for log anomaly detection because of their ability to interpret semantic and contextual information. However, LLM-based approaches can be affected by prompt construction, noisy log data, and reliance on generic datasets that may lack endpoint-specific authentication behaviours. To address these limitations, this study develops a standardised instruction-based LLM classification framework for detecting anomalous authentication behaviours, including borderline cases. A controlled cybersecurity testbed was developed to generate endpoint-specific authentication data, producing a curated dataset comprising normal, borderline, and anomalous behavioural scenarios. Three instruction-tuned LLMs, Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct, Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct, and GPT-OSS 20B, were evaluated against Wazuh rule-based detection and OpenSearch Anomaly Detection using a common ground-truth severity framework. Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct achieved the strongest overall end-to-end detection performance, with an accuracy of 89.3%, recall of 88.2%, F1-score of 91.8%, and false negative rate of 11.8%. In comparison, Wazuh achieved an accuracy of 52.0% and false negative rate of 68.6%, while OpenSearch achieved an accuracy of 49.3% and false negative rate of 74.5%. Meta Llama also detected 80% of the borderline anomalous scenarios, compared with 20% for Wazuh and 15% for OpenSearch. Qwen achieved lower overall detection performance than Meta Llama but recorded the lowest average inference latency and 100% structured-response validity. GPT-OSS demonstrated strong classification performance when valid responses were produced.

</details>


### [119] [Enforcing LLM Safety through DMD-based Classification of Prompt-Response Embedding Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19579)
*Mohamed Akrout,Olivera Kotevska,Dan Wilson*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Koopman动力学框架的黑盒LLM安全分类方法，通过分别拟合安全/不安全场景的预测模型并计算差分残差分数，实现无需内部访问的毒性检测，并在三个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在关键场景中可能生成有害内容，黑盒条件下的安全检测仍具挑战；现有方法多依赖静态分类，缺乏对提示-响应交互动态的建模。

Method: 将提示和响应投影到高维嵌入空间，为安全与不安全两类场景分别拟合Koopman算子，通过比较两类预测误差的差分残差分数进行安全分类，并引入提示嵌入以捕获交互模式。

Result: 在三个安全基准上，结合提示嵌入一致提升性能：交互依赖违规（如Llama-3因果解码器）效果更佳，而纯响应违规更适合稠密语义嵌入。

Conclusion: Koopman动力学框架可有效用于LLM安全分析，开辟了‘用动力学系统分析AI’的新方向，而非仅用AI建模动力学系统。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications, yet their tendency to generate toxic, harmful, or policy-violating content poses significant risks. Detecting these unsafe outputs efficiently in a black-box manner remains an open challenge.
